- **reset**: Moves the current branch (or detached `HEAD`) to `&lt;rev&gt;`, which defaults to `HEAD`. - **resilience-audit**: Reliability / failure-mode audit — why distributed and async systems fall over: single points of failure, missing timeouts, retries without backoff/idempotency, the dual-write problem, missing dead-letter queues, broker/queue at-least-once semantics, cron-job overlap & missed runs, circuit breakers, graceful degradation, health checks & graceful shutdown, backpressure, outbox pattern.
